Title: A Candid Narrative of the Rise and Progress of the Herrnhuters, commonly call'd Moravians or Unitas Fratrum, with a short Account of their Doctrines drawn from their own Writings; to which are added Observations on their Politics in general, and particularly on their Conduct whilst in the County of Büdingen
Creator: Henry Rimius, 'Aulic Counsellor to His late Majesty the King of Prussia'
Publisher: London: printed for A. Linde in Catherine Street in the Strand, and sold by J. Robinson, Mrs Cook and J. Barnes
Publication Date: 1753
Rights: Public Domain (published 1753, more than 95 years ago; public domain by date under the project's rolling rule; the item carries no rights field, so the date rule alone applies)
Source: Internet Archive item candidnarrativeo00rimi (https://archive.org/details/candidnarrativeo00rimi), the item's own candidnarrativeo00rimi_djvu.txt full-text OCR derivative; contributing library per the item metadata: John Carter Brown Library; host metadata date: 1753. Fetched directly (curl/urllib, raw bytes) by the Library thread (Era VIII pass), 2026-10-23. sha256 of the raw fetched bytes: dfd41da6f590ab6ea40860a69af648ea38cb9723bff5f37fc52db55012f62c7e.
Provenance note: Header prepended by the Library thread; nothing else altered, no OCR error corrected, nothing retyped. John Carter Brown Library scan; readable with the long s rendered as f by the OCR.
Content note: The most damaging English attack on the Brethren, dedicated to the Archbishop of Canterbury, quoting the German hymnals and Zinzendorf's discourses of the 1740s against them; the hostile witness whose quotations the Brethren had to answer, and did.
